i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (bset.test(i)) {\n      // Position counting from right (LSB=1), so position is i+1\n      return static_cast<uint64_t>(i + 1);\n    }\n  }\n  // If all zeros, return BITSET_CAPACITY (max position)\n  return BITSET_CAPACITY;\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Adds a value into the HyperLogLog.\n *\n * @param[in] val - value that's added into hyperloglog\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n  std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> binary = ComputeBinary(hash);\n\n  // Extract the bucket index from the first n_bits_ bits (MSB)\n  // The bucket index is taken from the most significant n_bits_ bits\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  for (int i = 0; i < n_bits_; i++) {\n    if (binary.test(BITSET_CAPACITY - 1 - i)) {\n      bucket_idx |= (static_cast<uint64_t>(1) << (n_bits_ - 1 - i));\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Create a mask to get the remaining bits (excluding the bucket index bits)\n  // We need to find the position of the leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n  std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> remaining_bits;\n  for (int i = 0; i < BITSET_CAPACITY - n_bits_; i++) {\n    remaining_bits[i] = binary[i];\n  }\n\n  // Find position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t position = 0;\n  for (int i = BITSET_CAPACITY - n_bits_ - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (remaining_bits.test(i)) {\n      position = static_cast<uint64_t>(BITSET_CAPACITY - n_bits_ - i);\n      break;\n    }\n  }\n  if (position == 0) {\n    position = BITSET_CAPACITY - n_bits_ + 1;\n  }\n\n  // Update the register with max value (thread-safe)\n  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);\n  if (position > registers_[bucket_idx]) {\n    registers_[bucket_idx] = position;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Function that computes cardinality.\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(registers_[i]));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  // where alpha = 0.79402 (CONSTANT), m = num_buckets_\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<size_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<std::string>;\n\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":331,"cache_read_input_tokens":36111,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":331,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":6,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b412d3b9-11e5-4fb0-9c40-bfbcb3757589","uuid":"55e8f029-f39a-485f-b277-07fd0a64b203"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01PdWwDyihseBPiG3seyqC2R","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01S1xRjWXNtQnPgvVat1zmiv","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/bustub/src/primer/hyperloglog_presto.cpp","content":"//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//\n//\n//                         BusTub\n//\n// hyperloglog_presto.cpp\n//\n// Identification: src/primer/hyperloglog_presto.cpp\n//\n// Copyright (c) 2015-2025, Carnegie Mellon University Database Group\n//\n//===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//\n\n#include \"primer/hyperloglog_presto.h\"\n#include <cmath>\n\nnamespace bustub {\n\n/** @brief Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits)\n    : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  // Handle negative bits - treat as invalid, use 0 buckets\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    num_buckets_ = 0;\n  } else {\n    num_buckets_ = static_cast<size_t>(1) << n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n  dense_bucket_.resize(num_buckets_, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // For Presto HLL, we use the hash directly (64 bits)\n  // Extract bucket index from the leading n_leading_bits_ bits (MSB)\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros in the remaining bits + 1 (position of leftmost 1)\n  // The remaining bits start after the bucket index bits\n  // Create mask to clear the bucket index bits\n  uint64_t remaining_bits = hash;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0 && n_leading_bits_ < 64) {\n    // Clear the top n_leading_bits_ bits\n    remaining_bits = hash << n_leading_bits_;\n  } else if (n_leading_bits_ >= 64) {\n    remaining_bits = 0;\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros and add 1 for the position\n  uint64_t position = 1;\n  if (remaining_bits == 0) {\n    // All zeros - maximum position\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_ + 1;\n  } else {\n    // Count leading zeros in remaining_bits\n    // __builtin_clzll counts leading zeros for 64-bit\n    int leading_zeros = __builtin_clzll(remaining_bits);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(leading_zeros + 1);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n  // Max value storable: (7 << 4) | 15 = 127\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                                     // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;           // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":331,"cache_read_input_tokens":40590,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":331,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b412d3b9-11e5-4fb0-9c40-bfbcb3757589","uuid":"0452175f-56ed-4670-b316-3a426bf5d1a6"}

Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits)\n    : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  // Handle negative bits - treat as invalid, use 0 buckets\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    num_buckets_ = 0;\n  } else {\n    num_buckets_ = static_cast<size_t>(1) << n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n  dense_bucket_.resize(num_buckets_, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // For Presto HLL, we use the hash directly (64 bits)\n  // Extract bucket index from the leading n_leading_bits_ bits (MSB)\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros in the remaining bits + 1 (position of leftmost 1)\n  // The remaining bits start after the bucket index bits\n  // Create mask to clear the bucket index bits\n  uint64_t remaining_bits = hash;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0 && n_leading_bits_ < 64) {\n    // Clear the top n_leading_bits_ bits\n    remaining_bits = hash << n_leading_bits_;\n  } else if (n_leading_bits_ >= 64) {\n    remaining_bits = 0;\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros and add 1 for the position\n  uint64_t position = 1;\n  if (remaining_bits == 0) {\n    // All zeros - maximum position\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_ + 1;\n  } else {\n    // Count leading zeros in remaining_bits\n    // __builtin_clzll counts leading zeros for 64-bit\n    int leading_zeros = __builtin_clzll(remaining_bits);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(leading_zeros + 1);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n  // Max value storable: (7 << 4) | 15 = 127\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                                     // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;           // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":11,"oldLines":23,"newStart":11,"newLines":112,"lines":[" i >= 0; i--) {\n    if (bset.test(i)) {\n      // Position is how many bits from MSB (1-indexed)\n      // bit 63 -> position 1, bit 62 -> position 2, etc.\n      return static_cast<uint64_t>(BITSET_CAPACITY - i);\n    }\n  }\n  // If all zeros, return BITSET_CAPACITY + 1 (max position)\n  return BITSET_CAPACITY + 1;\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Adds a value into the HyperLogLog.\n *\n * @param[in] val - value that's added into hyperloglog\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // Extract the bucket index from the first n_bits_ bits (MSB)\n  // The bucket index is taken from the most significant n_bits_ bits\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = hash >> (BITSET_CAPACITY - n_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Create remaining bits by shifting out the bucket index bits\n  // The remaining bits are used to count the position of leftmost 1\n  uint64_t remaining_hash = hash;\n  if (n_bits_ > 0 && n_bits_ < BITSET_CAPACITY) {\n    remaining_hash = hash << n_bits_;\n  } else if (n_bits_ >= BITSET_CAPACITY) {\n    remaining_hash = 0;\n  }\n\n  std::bitset<BITSET_CAPACITY> remaining_bits(remaining_hash);\n\n  // Find position of leftmost 1 in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t position = PositionOfLeftmostOne(remaining_bits);\n\n  // Update the register with max value (thread-safe)\n  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(mutex_);\n  if (position > registers_[bucket_idx]) {\n    registers_[bucket_idx] = position;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * @brief Function that computes cardinality.\n */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLog<KeyType>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(registers_[i]));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  // where alpha = 0.79402 (CONSTANT), m = num_buckets_\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<size_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLog<std::string>;\n\n}  // namespace bustub\n","numLines":132,"startLine":1,"totalLines":132}}}

HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits)\n    : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  // Handle negative bits - treat as invalid, use 0 buckets\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    num_buckets_ = 0;\n  } else {\n    num_buckets_ = static_cast<size_t>(1) << n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n  dense_bucket_.resize(num_buckets_, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // For Presto HLL, we use the hash directly (64 bits)\n  // Extract bucket index from the leading n_leading_bits_ bits (MSB)\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros in the remaining bits + 1 (position of leftmost 1)\n  // The remaining bits start after the bucket index bits\n  // Create mask to clear the bucket index bits\n  uint64_t remaining_bits = hash;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0 && n_leading_bits_ < 64) {\n    // Clear the top n_leading_bits_ bits\n    remaining_bits = hash << n_leading_bits_;\n  } else if (n_leading_bits_ >= 64) {\n    remaining_bits = 0;\n  }\n\n  // Count leading zeros and add 1 for the position\n  uint64_t position = 1;\n  if (remaining_bits == 0) {\n    // All zeros - maximum position\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_ + 1;\n  } else {\n    // Count leading zeros in remaining_bits\n    // __builtin_clzll counts leading zeros for 64-bit\n    int leading_zeros = __builtin_clzll(remaining_bits);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(leading_zeros + 1);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n  // Max value storable: (7 << 4) | 15 = 127\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                                     // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;           // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}

Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits)\n    : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  // Handle negative bits - treat as invalid, use 0 buckets\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    num_buckets_ = 0;\n  } else {\n    num_buckets_ = static_cast<size_t>(1) << n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n  dense_bucket_.resize(num_buckets_, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // For Presto HLL:\n  // - Bucket index comes from the LEAST significant n_leading_bits_ bits\n  // - Position is based on trailing zeros + 1 in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  uint64_t remaining_hash = hash;\n\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    // Extract bucket index from LSB\n    bucket_idx = hash & ((1ULL << n_leading_bits_) - 1);\n    // Remaining bits are the upper bits (shift right to remove bucket index bits)\n    remaining_hash = hash >> n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n\n  // Count trailing zeros in remaining_hash and add 1 for position\n  uint64_t position;\n  if (remaining_hash == 0) {\n    // All zeros in remaining - maximum position\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_ + 1;\n  } else {\n    // Count trailing zeros using built-in function\n    int trailing_zeros = __builtin_ctzll(remaining_hash);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(trailing_zeros + 1);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                           // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;  // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":55,"oldLines":7,"newStart":55,"newLines":7,"lines":["   uint64_t position;","   if (remaining_hash == 0) {"," negative bits - treat as invalid, use 0 buckets\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    num_buckets_ = 0;\n  } else {\n    num_buckets_ = static_cast<size_t>(1) << HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // For Presto HLL:\n  // - Bucket index comes from the MOST significant n_leading_bits_ bits\n  // - Position is based on leading zeros in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Shift left to remove bucket bits, then count leading zeros\n  uint64_t shifted = hash << n_leading_bits_;\n\n  // Calculate position\n  uint64_t position;\n  if (shifted == 0) {\n    // All zeros in remaining - maximum position\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_;\n  } else {\n    // Count leading zeros and calculate position\n    int leading_zeros = __builtin_clzll(shifted);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(63 - n_leading_bits_ - leading_zeros);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                           // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;  // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits)\n    : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  // Handle negative bits - treat as invalid, use 0 buckets\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    num_buckets_ = 0;\n  } else {\n    num_buckets_ = static_cast<size_t>(1) << n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n  dense_bucket_.resize(num_buckets_, std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(0));\n}\n\n/** @brief Element is added for HLL calculation. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // For Presto HLL:\n  // - Bucket index comes from the LEAST significant n_leading_bits_ bits\n  // - Position is based on trailing zeros + 1 in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  uint64_t remaining_hash = hash;\n\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    // Extract bucket index from LSB\n    bucket_idx = hash & ((1ULL << n_leading_bits_) - 1);\n    // Remaining bits are the upper bits (shift right to remove bucket index bits)\n    remaining_hash = hash >> n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n\n  // Count trailing zeros in remaining_hash and add 1 for position\n  uint64_t position;\n  if (remaining_hash == 0) {\n    // All zeros in remaining - maximum position\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_;\n  } else {\n    // Count trailing zeros using built-in function\n    int trailing_zeros = __builtin_ctzll(remaining_hash);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(trailing_zeros + 1);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                           // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;  // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n","numLines":118,"startLine":1,"totalLines":118}}}

the LEAST significant n_leading_bits_ bits\n  // - Position is based on trailing zeros + 1 in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  uint64_t remaining_hash = hash;\n\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    // Extract bucket index from LSB\n    bucket_idx = hash & ((1ULL << n_leading_bits_) - 1);\n    // Remaining bits are the upper bits (shift right to remove bucket index bits)\n    remaining_hash = hash >> n_leading_bits_;\n  }\n\n  // Count trailing zeros in remaining_hash and add 1 for position\n  uint64_t position;\n  if (remaining_hash == 0) {\n    // All zeros in remaining - maximum position\n    position = 64 - n_leading_bits_;\n  } else {\n    // Count trailing zeros using built-in function\n    int trailing_zeros = __builtin_ctzll(remaining_hash);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(trailing_zeros + 1);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = dense_bucket_[bucket_idx].to_ullong();\n  uint64_t current_overflow = 0;\n  if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n    current_overflow = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                           // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;  // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n    if (overflow_bucket_.find(static_cast<uint16_t>(i)) != overflow_bucket_.end()) {\n      overflow_val = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(i)].to_ullong();\n    }\n    uint64_t register_val = (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<std::string>;\n}  // namespace bustub\n"}}

HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n (overflow_val << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | dense_val;\n    sum += std::pow(2.0, -static_cast<double>(register_val));\n  }\n\n  // HyperLogLog formula: E = alpha * m^2 / sum(2^(-M[j]))\n  double m = static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class Parameterized constructor. */\ntemplate <typename KeyType>\nHyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::HyperLogLogPresto(int16_t n_leading_bits)\n    : cardinality_(0), n_leading_bits_(n_leading_bits) {\n  // Handle negative bits - treat as invalid, use 0 buckets\n  if (n_leading_bits_ < 0) {\n    n_leading_bits_ = 0;\n    num_buckets_ = 0;\n  } else {\n    num_buckets_ = static_cast<size_t>(1) << HyperLogLogPresto<KeyType>::AddElem(KeyType val) -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  hash_t hash = CalculateHash(val);\n\n  // For Presto HLL:\n  // - Bucket index comes from the MOST significant n_leading_bits_ bits\n  // - Position is calculated from trailing zeros in the remaining bits\n  uint64_t bucket_idx = 0;\n  if (n_leading_bits_ > 0) {\n    bucket_idx = hash >> (64 - n_leading_bits_);\n  }\n\n  // Mask to get remaining bits (clear the bucket bits in MSB)\n  uint64_t mask = (n_leading_bits_ >= 64) ? 0 : ((1ULL << (64 - n_leading_bits_)) - 1);\n  uint64_t position = 64 - n_leading_bits_;\n  } else {\n    // Count trailing zeros\n    int trailing_zeros = __builtin_ctzll(remaining);\n    position = static_cast<uint64_t>(trailing_zeros);\n  }\n\n  // The dense bucket stores lower 4 bits, overflow stores upper 3 bits\n  // Total value is: (overflow << 4) | dense\n\n  // Get current value\n  uint64_t current_dense = overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)].to_ullong();\n  }\n  uint64_t current_value = (current_overflow << DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) | current_dense;\n\n  // Only update if new position is greater\n  if (position > current_value) {\n    // Split position into dense (lower 4 bits) and overflow (upper bits)\n    uint64_t new_dense = position & 0xF;                           // Lower 4 bits\n    uint64_t new_overflow = (position >> DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE) & 0x7;  // Next 3 bits\n\n    dense_bucket_[bucket_idx] = std::bitset<DENSE_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_dense);\n    overflow_bucket_[static_cast<uint16_t>(bucket_idx)] = std::bitset<OVERFLOW_BUCKET_SIZE>(new_overflow);\n  }\n}\n\n/** @brief Function to compute cardinality. */\ntemplate <typename T>\nauto HyperLogLogPresto<T>::ComputeCardinality() -> void {\n  if (num_buckets_ == 0) {\n    cardinality_ = 0;\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // Calculate harmonic mean of 2^(-register[i])\n  double sum = 0.0;\n  for (size_t i = 0; i < num_buckets_; i++) {\n    // Reconstruct the full value from dense and overflow buckets\n    uint64_t dense_val = dense_bucket_[i].to_ullong();\n    uint64_t overflow_val = 0;\n static_cast<double>(num_buckets_);\n  double estimate = CONSTANT * m * m / sum;\n\n  cardinality_ = static_cast<uint64_t>(estimate);\n}\n\ntemplate class HyperLogLogPresto<int64_t>;\ntemplate class
